# BE-DOC-02 documentation manifest and execution ledger

**CURRENT_VERIFIED — source baseline `cdeb1ca1037744d06995058af13d0582f6273c0a`, `huy-review`, 2026-10-13.** Source observation is not all-path runtime execution. Expected business behavior uses owner-confirmed decisions and assigned specs; implementation/test evidence has its own limits.

## Frozen assignment and safety

[Approved specification](11_Audit_and_Gaps/BE-DOC-02-SPEC.md). Branch huy-review; expected/current baseline cdeb1ca1037744d06995058af13d0582f6273c0a, initial working tree clean; predecessor audit commit is HEAD. Baseline hash manifest covers source and static declaration inventory; root is integrator, domain agents only write reserved new docs. Only this directory is staged/committed. No source/tests/config/migration/seed/V2/FE lock edits or remote publication.

## Execution plan and review focus

- [x] A: verify Git and audit ancestry/diff/source hashes, read authority and latest supersession.
- [x] B: inventory all tracked Backend C# declarations/API Explorer/DTO/EF model/config/migration/security/workers/tests with source/hash/lines.
- [x] C: trace all workflow actors/read IDs/versions/current permission/business effects/recovery and separate expected/current.
- [x] D: author all required pages and exact catalogs with additional machine evidence.
- [x] E: capture independent production operation OpenAPI; exclude test-only artifacts, preserve implemented wire.
- [x] F: source↔docs cross-check; two self-review passes and read-only peer feedback; fix only docs.
- [x] G: build/focused tests, schema/OpenAPI/link/hash/scope checks; record failures and unselected NOT_RUN.
- [x] H: final scoped diff and commit contents reviewed; local commit/final SHA and post-commit clean-tree proof are recorded in external handoff to avoid self-reference.

Review focus: explicit web scheme vs legacy cookie whitelist; factory/default constructor/shared-core calls; inactive legacy implementations vs actual production DI; latest persistent login/ND01 vs historical rules; deployed/live acceptance vs source/test evidence. Checks/evidence are recorded in audit report and validation-evidence.json.

## Named authorization policies

| Policy | Registration/source | Actual requirement |
|---|---|---|
| project_work_package_read | API/Extensions/ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:84–88; Authorization/ProjectAccessAuthorization.cs:12 | Authenticated + ProjectAccessRequirement; handler→ProjectScopeGuard, current role/project membership or explicit Supervisor bypass |
| AiCallback | API/Extensions/ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:89–94 | AI service JWT scheme; authenticated and client_type=AI_SERVICE |

Default authenticated selector RoadGuardUser is an authentication policy scheme, distinct from authorization policies. Method/class Authorize and WebCookieEligible plus legacy whitelist are cataloged per operation; business guards remain in services/repositories.
